That's all.\n\nDoctor: Do you have any thoughts or beliefs about why the water is running or why those men are whispering, that you think might not be true?\n\nPatient: I don't know about... what's true or not. I know how it sounds. But... I think it's the mold. And the wiring in that house. \n\nI'm pretty sure it's poisoned everything I own. And... I can tell the neighbors are watching. They're just waiting to see when I mess up again. That's all.\n\nDoctor: When you say the mold and wiring are poisoning everything, and that the neighbors are watching you, do you believe these things are definitely true, even though others might not agree with you?\n\nPatient: ...Yeah. I know it probably sounds far-fetched to you. But... I'm certain. \n\nThe mold and the wiring... they really have poisoned everything I own. And... I just know the neighbors are watching. Waiting for me to mess up. I don't really care if anyone else agrees with me. I just know it.\n\nDoctor: Have you been feeling down, hopeless, or losing interest in things you used to enjoy?\n\nPatient: Yeah... I guess. Most days, I just... I wake up already feeling like I've failed. At being a husband, and... just a man. It's just this heaviness. It just sits on me until I go to bed. So... yeah.\n\nDoctor: Have you lost interest in activities you used to enjoy, or found that you no longer take pleasure in them?\n\nPatient: ...Yeah. I don't really do much anymore. \n\nI used to be the guy who... showed up for things. Played soccer, brewed beer out in the garage... helped out at the fire department. But... I don't know. It all just feels pointless now. I can't make myself care enough to go. So I don't.\n\n[Instruction]\n- Ask about exactly ONE thing. Never bundle several symptoms, or several attributes of one symptom, into a single question — e.g. asking whether a symptom is present and how long it has lasted at the same time.\n- If you need to ask about more than one thing, ask the one that tells you the most now and come back to the rest later.\n- Keep the question clear enough for the patient to understand.\n- Respond in English.\n\n[Output Format — JSON only, no other text]\n{\"intent\": \"<what you want to find out with this question>\", \"question\": \"<how you actually ask it in the interview>\"}\n"
